fae_heart_of_glade_01 = {
	slot_type = internal
	construction_time = @camp_building_main_slot_construction_duration_t1
	allowed_domicile_types = { camp haven_landed }
	previous_building = camp_main_01

	cost = { gold = @fae_domicile_building_cost_t1 }
	refund = {
		gold = {
			value = @fae_domicile_building_refound_t1
			multiply = camp_refund_mult_value
			floor = yes
		}
	}

	can_construct = {
		domicile_building_requirement_fae = yes
	}
	can_construct_potential = {
		domicile_building_requirement_fae = yes
	}

	parameters = {
		every_6_years_gain_glamour = yes
	}

	character_modifier = {
		domicile_monthly_prestige_add = 0.5
	}

	ai_value = { value = camp_building_ai_base_t1 }

	allowed_domicile_types = { camp haven_landed }
	construction_time = @camp_building_internal_slot_construction_duration_t4
	previous_building = fae_the_interior_hamlet_03

	cost = {
		gold = @fae_domicile_internal_building_cost_t4
		prestige = @fae_domicile_internal_building_cost_t4
	}
	refund = {
		gold = {
			value = @fae_domicile_internal_building_refound_t4
			multiply = camp_refund_mult_value
			floor = yes
		}
		prestige = {
			value = @fae_domicile_internal_building_refound_t4
			multiply = camp_refund_mult_value
			floor = yes
		}
	}

	can_construct = {
		domicile_building_requirement_fae = yes
		domicile = { NOT = { has_domicile_building_or_higher = fae_the_interior_04 } }
	}
	can_construct_potential = {
		domicile_building_requirement_fae = yes
	}

	parameters = {
		fae_the_interior_hamlet_04_second_aptitude = yes
		fae_the_interior_hamlet_04_antiquarian_aptitude = yes
		fae_the_interior_hamlet_04_seneschal_aptitude = yes
		fae_the_interior_hamlet_04_councilor = yes
		fae_the_interior_hamlet_04_mortal = yes
	}

	on_complete = {
		POD_fae_hamlet_councillor_pulse_effect = { SCALE = 3 }
		POD_fae_hamlet_mortal_courtier_pulse_effect = { SCALE = 2 }
	}

	ai_value = { value = 0 }

	asset = {
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_fixed_layout.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/estate_building_workshop_mena.dds"
		intersectionmask_texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/estate_building_workshop_mena_mask.png"
		soundeffect = "event:/DLC/EP3/SFX/UI/domicile_buildings/ep3_ui_domicile_buildings_workshop_textile"
	}
}

fae_the_interior_training_ground_of_marshal_01 = {
	slot_type = internal
	allowed_domicile_types = { camp haven_landed }
	construction_time = @camp_building_internal_slot_construction_duration_t1
	previous_building = fae_the_interior_01

	cost = {
		gold = @fae_domicile_internal_building_cost_t1
		prestige = @fae_domicile_internal_building_cost_t1
	}
	refund = {
		gold = {
			value = @fae_domicile_internal_building_refound_t1
			multiply = camp_refund_mult_value
			floor = yes
		}
		prestige = {
			value = @fae_domicile_internal_building_refound_t1
			multiply = camp_refund_mult_value
			floor = yes
		}
	}

	can_construct = {
		domicile_building_requirement_fae = yes
	}
	can_construct_potential = {
		domicile_building_requirement_fae = yes
		var:chosen_fable ?= flag:tale_of_marshal
	}

	character_modifier = {
		advantage = 1
	}

	ai_value = { value = camp_building_ai_base_t1 }

	asset = {
		icon = "gfx/interface/icons/domicile_building/domicile_building_barracks.dds"
		texture = "gfx/interface/window_domiciles/estate_building_workshop_mena.dds"

	}
